# The tests of the subset expected to fail, each with the reason: the
# path, white space, then the reason; a testharness subtest by its test's
# path, a tab, its name, a tab and the reason, the name in printable
# ASCII with \u{HEX} for any other character. A test or subtest that
# fails without being named here fails the run, and so does one named
# here that passes, unless its reason begins `either:`: its outcome
# depends on the machine's timing, and it may pass.
# The steps are those of the milestone a section names, in its design
# note's section 3: docs/design/flexbox.md for M17, docs/design/grid.md
# for M18, docs/design/compositing.md for M19, docs/design/media.md for
# M20, docs/design/values.md for M21, docs/design/animations.md for M22,
# docs/design/images.md for M23, docs/design/dom-scripting.md for M25,
# docs/design/events.md for M26.

# Not in either milestone (docs/design/flexbox.md and docs/design/grid.md,
# section 2).

css/css-flexbox/fixed-table-layout-with-percentage-width-in-flex-item.html               a fixed-layout table with a percentage width has an unbounded max-content width in the reference browsers, in a float as in a flex item; this engine's is its columns'

css/css-flexbox/flexbox-definite-sizes-004.html                                          the body's overflow is not carried to the viewport (css-overflow-3 3.3), so the body is a formatting context root here and its child's top margin does not collapse through it

# Not in M19 (docs/design/compositing.md, section 2): the planes of a 3D
# rendering context are painted whole by the depth of their centres, with
# no splitting where they cross; a turned, skewed or reflected box is
# resampled from a layer of its own plane rather than rasterized through
# its transform as the reference browser rasterizes it.
css/css-transforms/css-transform-3d-transform-style.html                       the two planes of the context cross along the box's middle, and each shows in front of the other on its side of the line; painted whole by their depth, the child's plane covers the parent's
css/css-transforms/transform3d-sorting-006.html                                the two planes cross, and each shows in front of the other on its side of the line; painted whole by their depth, one covers the other
css/css-transforms/ttwf-css-3d-polygon-cycle.html                              the three planes form a cycle, each over the next; painted whole by their depth, no order of them is right
css/css-transforms/scale-transform-overlap.html                                a `will-change: transform` box inside a scaled group is a layer resampled under the scale, where the reference draws it through: 340 pixels differ by up to 33 levels
css/css-transforms/transform3d-perspective-001.html                            a turned box is resampled from a layer of its own plane: 7 pixels differ from the reference by one level, where the test allows none

# Where the reference browser, the draft and the web-platform-tests do not
# agree (docs/design/values.md, section 1: the reference browser and the
# tests together over the draft, else the draft), or the tests disagree
# with themselves.
css/css-lists/counter-list-item-2.html                                 the web-platform-tests disagree with themselves: this one wants the nested `ol`'s `list-item` counter passed on to the `li` after it, as the draft has it, where counters-scope-001 wants a nested counter of the same name ended with its element, as the reference browser has it and this engine follows (design note, counters), giving 3 here as the reference browser does
css/css-lists/counters-scope-004.html                                  the test wants the counter a `::before` resets kept to the `::before` (1 1 R 2 3); the draft passes it on to the elements after the `::before` in its element, and so do the reference browser and this engine (1 1 R 1.1 1.1)
css/css-lists/counter-reset-reversed-siblings-003.html                 the test wants the reversed counter the inner div instantiates, inside the outer div's of the same name, passed on to the div after it, as the draft has it (4 2); this engine ends it with its element, as the reference browser ends such a counter (counters-scope-001), and gives 1 8; the reference browser, with no `reversed()` outside `list-item`, gives 9 7
css/css-lists/marker-counter.html                                      the counter properties do not apply to `::marker` (Lists 3 §3.1.1 lists the properties that do), as in the reference browser, which renders the test as this engine does
css/css-text-decor/text-decoration-thickness-length-rounding-002.html  a length thickness is rounded to the nearest whole pixel, 2.7px to 3, as in the reference browser; the tentative test wants it floored to 2
css/CSS2/generated-content/content-counter-004.xht                     the counters are the text ▪ of the `square` style, drawn by the font as the reference browser draws them, where the reference's inside markers are `square` shapes, smaller, as the reference browser draws those too

# Gaps outside M21, named at their milestones.
css/css-values/viewport-units-scrollbars-auto-vhw-001.html  the viewport scrolls only vertically (horizontal scrolling is M26), so it has no horizontal scrollbar, and the body's bottom border at 100vh shows where the reference browser's horizontal scrollbar covers it
css/CSS2/generated-content/content-048.xht                  HTML's `bgcolor` attribute on `body`, a presentational hint, is not one the cascade has (presentational attributes, a gap stated at M12), so the page is not yellow; `attr(bgcolor)` writes the text as it should
css/CSS2/generated-content/content-145.xht                  HTML's `text` attribute on `body`, a presentational hint for `color`, is not one the cascade has (presentational attributes, a gap stated at M12), so the word `attr(text)` writes is black, not green

# Gaps outside M22, named at their milestones, or at none.

# Tests taken at M31 (docs/design/workers.md, step 8): the testharness.js
# tests of workers, webmessaging and the structured clone's directories,
# the worker variants of every .any.js test the subset takes and the
# canvas's .worker.js tests, but shared, service and module workers,
# blob: URLs, SharedArrayBuffer and BroadcastChannel (examples/wpt.rs).
# A worker variant fails as its window test does, by the same gap; the
# rest by a gap named at its milestone, where the reference browser
# and the test differ, or what the runner's one server cannot serve.
